{"antonyms":[{"word":"copulator"},{"word":"libertine"}],"forms":[{"form":"virgins","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A person who has never had sexual intercourse, or (uncommonly) an animal that has never mated."],"synonyms":[{"word":"maiden"},{"word":"unicorn bait"},{"word":"virgo intacta"},{"word":"vestal"}]},{"glosses":["A person who has never engaged in any sexual activity at all."]},{"examples":[{"text":"In its list of saints, the Catholic Church classifies St. Agnes, St. Maria Goretti, and St. Thérèse of Lisieux as virgins.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Someone vowed to virginity (usually a woman and often a consecrated virgin), or someone who died in defense of their virginity; (especially) one venerated as a saint."],"qualifier":"Orthodoxy or historical","topics":["Catholicism","Christianity"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I've never eaten tofu before—you could say I'm a tofu virgin.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who has never used or experienced a specified thing."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["Any of several species of gossamer-winged butterflies of the family Lycaenidae."]},{"glosses":["A female insect producing eggs from which young are hatched, though there has been no fecundation by a male; a parthenogenetic insect."],"topics":["biology","entomology","natural-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈvɜːd͡ʒɪn/","tags":["Australia","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈvɝd͡ʒɪn/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈvøːd͡ʒɪn/","tags":["New-Zealand","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/ˈvɪɾd͡ʒɪn/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/veːd͡ʒɪn/"},{"ipa":"/vɛːd͡ʒɪn/"}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"maid"},{"tags":["humorous","slang"],"word":"unicorn bait"},{"word":"virgin"}],"upstream_line":214061,"upstream_offset":1035606398,"upstream_record_sha256":"69286c1c4c99739931ed737537d604d701b073e260354c41501aaca29478c44c","word":"virgin"}
